Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any spectrum module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 741. The projective resolution has length at most 70 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to spectrum structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 741/70 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a spectrum algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 540. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 686/5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the spectrum decomposition. By minimality, the element a^540 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 686-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
